#### Batch A (39-01, 39-03, 39-06)

# Dispositions, planner A (39-01, 39-03, 39-06), referee-one review c1

- 39-01-H1, HIGH, fixed: the generator collision with 39-03 is removed by the orchestrator's wave move (39-03 to wave 2, 39-06 to wave 3); both plans keep staging exactly what `tools/gen_test_groups.py:96-104` changes, per the orchestrator's rule.
- 39-01-H2, HIGH, fixed: `resolve_role` now skips a matching seed row without a seed role (never raises; new behavior case); `load_seed` stays strict for the build-time tests, and the plan states the scan contract for 39-04: catch `OSError`/`ValueError` from `load_seed` and `config_io.ConfigError` from `user_roles`, continue with `[]`/`{}`, notes `skill seed ignored: <error>` and `skills.roles ignored: <error>` (truth line and Artifacts).
- 39-01-M1, MEDIUM, fixed in part: the duplicate check hashes `(name, kind)` only when both are strings (new planted `name: ["x"]` case); rejected that the guard must prove arbitrary fields free of model names: the plan map (LOCKED) names the repo's guard helper `scan_text` (`tests/test_arch_hardcoded_models.py:170`), whose coverage is the repo-wide standard, and the tree guard also scans the shipped seed file.
- 39-01-M2, MEDIUM, fixed in part: `user_roles` now rejects `unsorted` (valid stated roles are the seven without it), matching 39-08's confirm writer and 39-16's docs; deferred to 39-15 (planner F): its one lock test should also compare the seed file's top-level `roles` list, and its generator step must stage what `gen_test_groups.py` changes (orchestrator rule b).
- 39-01-M3, MEDIUM, fixed in part: the 100-column rule now applies to Python files only and the seed JSON keeps one row per line; the `seed_problems` signature line is 93 columns, so its grep holds; rejected the isolation part: the bare `make ruff/pyright/vulture/pylint` gates are mandated by planner-common.md and read no HOME config, and every test run already goes through the scratch-HOME wrapper.
- 39-01-P1 (ponytail Task 1), HIGH, fixed: one owner per wave for generated state through the wave move.
- 39-01-P2 (ponytail Task 2), MEDIUM, rejected: the injected `literal_hits` checker is the only way to keep the map's literal check inside `seed_problems` without the package importing `tests/` (map row 39-01, LOCKED); recorded in planner-notes-a.md.
- 39-01-P3 (ponytail Task 3), MEDIUM, fixed: read-only reuse of `config_io.cfg_layered_table` kept; persisted `unsorted` semantics settled (rejected on read).
- 39-03-H1, HIGH, deferred to 39-12 (planner E), 39-10 (planner D), contract fixed here: 39-03 now states the caller contract (pass each embedded value unjoined and as embedded, one position per path, a missing list as-is, the run directory only for the MCP value); which values 39-12 and 39-10 pass and the dispatch-seam tests belong to those plans.
- 39-03-H2, HIGH, fixed: `_shown` redacts with `secret_shapes.redact(value, "[redacted]")` (`secret_shapes.py:99`, the marker of `usability.py:23`) before escaping; new test `test_shown_redacts_secrets`; threat row T-39-03-5.
- 39-03-M1, MEDIUM, fixed in part: added `test_non_nfc_name_compared_as_given`; rejected the missing-component claim: once a component is missing nothing below it exists, so the non-strict fallback can only ignore missing components (a loop or a path through a file raises a non-`FileNotFoundError` `OSError` under strict and stays `path_unresolvable`); rejected the git-env point: `project_root` runs git in the caller's own environment, where an inherited `GIT_DIR` is the owner's statement of the repository, and tests fake git (`gsd_tools.py:34-48`).
- 39-03-H3, HIGH, fixed in part: the generator collision is removed by the wave move; rejected the wrapper point: the fixture generator only writes a repository file (no HOME, no node, no host) and the make gates are mandated bare by planner-common.md.
- 39-03-P1 (ponytail Task 1), HIGH, fixed: reuses `gsd_tools.project_root` and now `secret_shapes.redact`; the dispatch coverage part is deferred to 39-12 as in 39-03-H1.
- 39-03-P2 (ponytail Task 2), MEDIUM, fixed: fixture and filesystem tests extended in place (two new tests), no second fixture or validator.
- 39-06-H1, HIGH, deferred to 39-12 (planner E), contract fixed here: `window_for` takes the boolean; 39-06 now states that the caller passes True when the final argv carries a context form: `setting context=` applied, or `ignored reason=id_carries` (`cross-ai.mjs:2015`, `:2036`); the composer-seam case belongs to 39-12.
- 39-06-H2, HIGH, deferred to 39-11 (planner D) and 39-12 (planner E), contract fixed here: budget functions take `window["value"]` (int or None), the dict is for display and the refusal, and the block prints the unknown value and its note.
- 39-06-M1, MEDIUM, fixed: a catalog label outside `catalog_labels.LABELS` (`catalog_labels.py:9`) shows `unknown` (the integer still caps, SPEC R7 adjacency edge) and the source is redacted with `secret_shapes.redact`; new test `test_catalog_provenance_checked`.
- 39-06-M2, MEDIUM, fixed in part: Task 3 adds a node preflight script (`node_check.py`, non-zero when the real node is missing) before the test run; the staging text now names each task's own `git add` line; rejected the width point: the `window_for` signature is 96 columns and fits on one line.
- 39-06-L1, LOW, fixed: with 39-06 in wave 3 and 39-05 in wave 2 the read/write race on `tests/test_prompt_guard.py` is gone.
- 39-06-P1 (ponytail Task 1), MEDIUM, fixed: the private `runner_dispatch._row_matches` import and the sorted loop are replaced by the public `usability.find_runtime_key` (`usability.py:45-57`).
- 39-06-P2 (ponytail Task 2), HIGH, deferred to 39-12 (planner E): the real final-argv case belongs at the composer seam; the pi unit case stays as the helper's contract test.
- 39-06-P3 (ponytail Task 3), MEDIUM, fixed: reuses `runner_facts.read_runner_describe`; the skip is now caught by the preflight.
- ORCH-BUILTIN, (orchestrator decision), fixed: 39-01's `simplify` seed row gains `"built_in": "claude"` (SPEC R5 built-in lens with no file); `seed_problems` accepts `built_in` only on a `skill` row naming one of `BUILT_IN_HOSTS = ("claude", "codex", "opencode", "cursor", "copilot", "pi")` (texts `built_in on a non-skill row`, `built_in '<value>' is not a host`); new test `test_built_in_only_on_skill_rows`; 39-11 reads the field.
- CROSS-R2, (cross-plan), deferred to 39-08 (planner C): concurrent confirms need serialization beyond atomic replace.
- CROSS-R5, (cross-plan), deferred to 39-11 and 39-12 (planner D, E): checked, budgeted and delivered lens bytes must come from one snapshot per dispatch.

#### Batch B (39-02, 39-05, 39-17)

# Dispositions, planner B (39-02, 39-05, 39-17), referee-one review c1

- 39-02-H1, HIGH, fixed: the wire label is now ONE string `<state>: <source>` (`capLabel`), matching 39-07's `_well_formed` string check (39-07-PLAN.md:132, :159); rows keep `{state, source}` for `--describe`; the harness and Python tests pin `{value, label}` with a string label (must_haves "Wire schema").
- 39-02-H1 (superseded by the orchestrator's final decision), HIGH, fixed: the wire label is an OBJECT `{state, source}` (the row forms' shape), which 39-07 now validates and a string label fails; `capLabel` returns a fresh object, every case and the Python test read `.label.state` / `.label.source`; the fixture holds values only and the describe golden already holds object labels, so neither changes shape.
- 39-02-H2, HIGH, fixed: the describe golden is regenerated in Task 1 (pi, copilot) and Task 2 (claude, codex, cursor, opencode), each committed with the forms that change it; Task 3 commits only `tests/test_runner_skills_argv.py` and edits no other consumer (a failing reader stops the task).
- 39-02-H3, HIGH, fixed: `splitFlags` splits every `--flag=value` once before facts match (`--sandbox=read-only` case m2); differing repeated allow lists, conflicting facts, a fact against the tool list, a variadic flag with a separate value (copilot, claude `variadic: true`) and any `*` pattern other than bare `*` are `unknown` (cases g, m, q, r, t); no precedence is assumed (SPEC R3 ordering edge "where known").
- 39-02-H4, HIGH, fixed: cursor's `--sandbox disabled` write fact is removed (no help line states the reach, cursor/help.txt:46-48), so cursor exec is `write: unknown`; codex bypass plus `--sandbox read-only` is now `write: unknown`; cursor `--force` keeps `shell: yes` from its help line (help.txt:39), labeled verified.
- 39-02-M1, MEDIUM, fixed in part: the planted flip now reads `no` to `unknown`, and the real-dispatch refusal of the same plant is pinned by the existing `lock-late-usage-no-lock` (run in Task 2's verify; allowlist unchanged); rejected the "shared planner result" part: `planSettings` runs before the final argv exists (cross-ai.mjs:2122-2127, argv built at :7044-7046 and :3891) and Python reads the line through `--print-argv` (CONTEXT D-03), recorded as a LIVE FACT.
- 39-02-M2, MEDIUM, fixed in part: a LIVE FACT now states `max_bytes` bounds the composed element (preamble, prose blocks, prompt; cross-ai.mjs:3905-3909); the caller budget is deferred to 39-11/39-12; a real `--argv-file` preview is deferred (the pure case i pins the argv-file result; a live one needs a declared argv file outside this plan's scope).
- 39-02-PH1, HIGH (ponytail Task 1), fixed: the golden lands in the tracer's commit; no Python parser, the prompt limit reuses `ARGV_PROMPT_MAX` and `RESUME_MSG`.
- 39-02-PM2, MEDIUM (ponytail Task 2), fixed in part: the fixture is one lane per line (about 25 lines); both the harness table and the Python table stay, because plan-map.md:14 locks both.
- 39-02-PM3, MEDIUM (ponytail Task 3), fixed: Task 3 reuses `_SkillCase._run`; node runs through `selftest.sh --check`; the make gates run through `lint.sh` with `UV_OFFLINE=1`.
- 39-05-H1, HIGH, deferred to 39-11 and 39-12 (planners D and E): the caller must call 39-11's `check_lens` on the resolved directory so a rejected lens is a named `path_rejected` skip, not a runner usage exit; 39-05 keeps the runner refusal.
- 39-05-M1, MEDIUM, rejected: plan-map.md:17 keeps `CONTROL_CHAR_RE` for the runner's other flags and the locked SPEC R6 amendment limits the runner change to the skill flags; escaped display of embedded values is 39-03's `escape_value` and 39-12's refusal record; the MCP-path case pins only scope.
- 39-05-L1, LOW, fixed: `selftest.sh --check` (real node, scratch HOME) and `lint.sh` (UV_OFFLINE) replace bare `node --check` and `make`.
- 39-05-PL1, LOW (ponytail Task 1), no change: the reviewer endorses the parse-check reuse.
- 39-05-PM2, MEDIUM (ponytail Task 2), fixed: `cp = (e) => parseInt(e[0], 16)` reads 39-03's declared `[hex, name]` shape only (39-03-PLAN.md:157); Task 2 has an explicit precondition (Task 1 committed, 87/15 fixture pairs, `cap-print-argv-table` green).
- 39-17-H1, HIGH, fixed: a STOP RULE preserves both files when `.new` exists, `live` fails or the live digest is neither the pre-render nor the approved dry digest; rollback restores only bytes this task published (live digest equals the approved dry digest); no revert inside the task.
- 39-17-H2, HIGH, fixed: the orchestrator runs the dry render first and approves with a ledger line bound to `head=`, `live=` and `dry=` digests; Task 2 checks them; dogfood writes `p39-17 dogfood: ok|failed <sha>`, Task 3's precondition requires the ok line; a render-caused failure reverts the render and version commits together, any other cause is decided by the orchestrator.

#### Batch E (39-12, 39-13, 39-14)

# Dispositions, planner E (39-12, 39-13, 39-14), referee-one review c1

- 39-12-H1, HIGH, fixed: the tracer and Task 2 now call 39-10 `build_block(BlockInput(...))`, run every lens through 39-11 `check_lens` (runner name `skill_name`, real path, `bytes`) and pass `plan_lenses` the integer `window["value"]`; no ASSUMED marker remains (39-10-PLAN.md:127-138, 39-11-PLAN.md:140-141).
- 39-12-H2, HIGH, fixed in part: the preview result passes to `lane_facts` unchanged, so a failed preview keeps 39-07's `NO_PREVIEW_REASON` (new test `test_failed_preview_is_all_unknown_with_reason`); the label-shape question (string or `{state, source}`) is deferred to 39-02 and 39-07, which own the line and its reader; 39-02's artifact list gives string labels and 39-07's `TestLaneFactsFromTheRunner` reads a real runner line.
- 39-12-H3, HIGH, fixed in part: values pass unjoined (`validate_values` joins relative values to the repository root, 39-03-PLAN.md:32, :205); every `--files` value, each checked lens path, each skill path and the MCP file (run dir as an extra root for that value only) are validated; a dispatch-step test proves a rejected file never spawns (`test_rejected_file_never_spawns`); the two guidance paths are resolved and validated by the caller (`guidance_paths`, 39-10's `BlockInput.shared_path`/`agents_path`): a rejected one is not embedded and is named on stderr, the lane is not refused for it, because `AGENTS_TOOLING_PATH` normally resolves to `~/.agents/AGENTS-TOOLING.md` (detection.py:1359-1365), outside every repository and skill root; whether such a path should refuse the lane instead is deferred to the orchestrator; an absent `--files` stays "no file named" (rejected: the dispatch embeds no file value then); escaping and redaction of `shown` is 39-03's (deferred).
- 39-12-H4, HIGH, fixed: the final block, the delivered lens bytes and the argv cap are checked on what is really sent, and a lens that fits the draft but not the final prompt is skipped whole (`over_window`) before the lane is refused (`test_lens_skipped_not_lane_refused`, `test_argv_cap_skips_lens`); a blank prompt is not composed, so the runner's `empty_stdin` stays (`test_blank_prompt_is_not_composed`); an unknown window prints `ai-kit-spec: window for <runtime>/<model>: unknown (<note or label>); not capped` from the dispatch.
- 39-12-H5, HIGH, fixed in part: `capability-block --native` builds a native lane's lens lines through `BlockInput(facts=None)` (`test_native_lane_gets_lens_lines_only`); `LaneRefused` is now a ValueError subclass; the superpowers adapter has no catch around `dispatch_execute` (dispatch_injection.py:387, adapter cli.py:345) and is outside this plan's files: deferred to the orchestrator; `simplify` seeded as `edit_lens` versus `built_in_no_file` is 39-01's and 39-11's (deferred).
- 39-12-H6, HIGH, fixed: Task 1 imports only what it uses; the soft-confinement sentence keeps its wording and reaches the block through `LaneRequest.soft_confinement` -> `BlockInput.soft_confinement` (`ExecuteSeams.build_soft_confinement_fn` kept); the two confinement tests run the real composer and block with a write-keyed fake preview and keep every assert; the execute seam in tests/test_ai_kit_spec_runner_dispatch.py moved to Task 3; the runner e2e module runs after the execute switch.
- 39-12-M1, MEDIUM, fixed in part: the MCP directory is owned from the line after `mkdtemp` (`test_mcp_dir_removed_when_publication_fails`); the second preview is rejected as a D-03 conflict: D-03 keys the preview by (runtime, model, role, extras) and the lens and MCP flags are extras (39-CONTEXT.md:65-66); the "present, not enabled" line for an absent binary is 39-10's (deferred).

- 39-14-H2, HIGH, fixed: the record is claimed with an exclusive create in state `running` and published `ready` after the run; accept and reject refuse a running pass; reject never overwrites a file changed after the pass and keeps the record (`test_second_pass_cannot_claim`, `test_reject_keeps_owner_changes`).

## Cycle 2 (referee-one)

### Review conditions (cycle 2)

- Reviewer: referee-one only, opencode `router-env/my-referee-one`. Six batches over all 19 plans (every plan changed in the cycle 1
  fix pass), run in parallel 01:20-01:36, every lane exit 0 (prompts 308-323 KB: cycle 1 triage and the orchestrator's cross-plan
  decisions inlined).
- No cycle 3 (time rule). A targeted fix pass followed: each planner verified every HIGH concern against the code, fixed the verified
  ones, recorded refuted ones with evidence, and wrote `tmp/p39-review/c2/dispositions-<batch>.md` (copied into the triage below);
  live facts that make SPEC wording impossible or already satisfied went into dated 2026-11-23 SPEC notes ("plan fix pass").
- Then the plan checker ran with a full brief (task self-containment, shared names between plans, requirement coverage, edge and
  prohibition ownership).

### referee-one

#### Batch A (39-01, 39-03, 39-06)

#### 39-01

##### Summary

The plan gives REV-05 a clear foundation: one closed role list, one shipped seed, and one read-only resolver. The wave move fixes the generated-file collision, and the code/seed/SKILL.md lock has a downstream owner. The remaining blocker is that the promised fallback for bad seed or role data works in the scanner but not in the dispatch that consumes it.

##### Strengths

- The resolver separates classification from presence, matches seed rows by exact name and kind, and tests `user-stated > seed > unsorted`. The scan independently proves that an absent seed-listed skill does not appear. [39-01-PLAN.md:158–171](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-01-PLAN.md#L158), [39-04-PLAN.md:125–129](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-04-PLAN.md#L125)
- The plan reuses the repository’s layered config reader rather than adding another merger. The reader does merge per key, project over user. The three-way roles lock really is assigned to 39-15. `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/config_io.py:552–570`, [39-15-PLAN.md:273](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-15-PLAN.md#L273)
- No declared same-wave file overlap with 39-02. Both generated listings belong in the tracer commit: the generator rewrites both, and the gate compares both with fresh discovery. [39-01-PLAN.md:5–12](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-01-PLAN.md#L5), [39-02-PLAN.md:5–12](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-02-PLAN.md#L5), `tools/gen_test_groups.py:96–104`, `tests/test_gate_scope.py:570–583`

##### Concerns

- **HIGH — A malformed seed or role entry can still stop dispatch.** 39-01 promises that these faults will not block a lane, and 39-04 catches them in `scan`. But 39-11 calls `load_seed()` again in both lens selection and MCP selection, and 39-12 calls `user_roles()` again in `_mcp`. A bad seed file or a syntactically valid TOML role entry with an invalid value can therefore pass the scan with a note and then abort composition. A structurally damaged seed is also a problem: `load_seed` accepts any list, while downstream code assumes each row is a dict. [39-01-PLAN.md:30–31,170–171](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-01-PLAN.md#L30), [39-04-PLAN.md:141](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-04-PLAN.md#L141), [39-11-PLAN.md:138,171](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-11-PLAN.md#L138), [39-12-PLAN.md:201–205](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-12-PLAN.md#L201)
- **MEDIUM — A wrong-shaped roles table is silently treated as absent.** Task 3 validates entries only after `cfg_layered_table` has converted any non-table node into `{}`. A valid TOML file containing `[skills]` with a scalar `roles` key produces no error or scan note; the sorter’s strict pre-check likewise misses it. The tests cover a bad entry and broken TOML, not this case. [39-01-PLAN.md:235–242](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-01-PLAN.md#L235), `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/config_io.py:552–570`, [39-08-PLAN.md:139](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-08-PLAN.md#L139)
- **MEDIUM — Task 2’s prescribed launch predicate can fail its commit hook.** Written as one `if not (...)`, it has seven boolean terms. The installed pylint counts terms recursively and defaults to five; the repository does not disable that check, and the commit hook applies pylint to this runtime module. Wrapping the expression over more lines does not fix it. [39-01-PLAN.md:208–213](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-01-PLAN.md#L208), `pyproject.toml:70–95`, `.pre-commit-config.yaml:26–32`, `.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/pylint/checkers/design_analysis.py:222–233,406–413,669–682`

##### Suggestions

- Give the scanner and lane composer one validated seed/role loading path. Preserve the strict reader for config-time errors, but test the **real composition path** with a broken seed and a bad role entry. Check the table shape before the generic merge discards it.
- Specify the launch check as separate object, command, args, and source checks, with the same error text. Keep the existing hardcoding checker; its position-based behavior is the locked standard.

##### Risk Assessment

**HIGH.** The module is small and the tasks are bounded, but the current handoff breaks the explicit R1 prohibition that a seed or role fault must not block a lane. Task 1 also has a full unit/arch commit hook on its critical path; the stated timing leaves room in a 1,800-second task but does not establish room for repeated attempts. [39-01-PLAN.md:91](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-01-PLAN.md#L91), `tools/run_unittest_parallel.py:755–759`

##### Ponytail findings

- **LOW — Task 1:** The seed, resolver and generated registration are necessary. Reuse `tools/gen_test_groups.py:96–104`; no manual listing edits or second presence source are needed.
- **MEDIUM — Task 2:** Keep the injected repository guard as locked, but put structural validation on the actual load path too. A test-only checker does not make the values consumed at 39-11 safe to use. [39-01-PLAN.md:170,208–212](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-01-PLAN.md#L170)
- **MEDIUM — Task 3:** Keep the existing layer readers and the separate writer. Add shape validation at this boundary, not another TOML parser or writer. `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/config_io.py:552–570`, [39-08-PLAN.md:176–178](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-08-PLAN.md#L176)

#### 39-03

##### Summary

The plan covers the eight R8 validator fixtures, path-component containment, ordered rejections, and safe diagnostic display. The independent Unicode fixture has a matching consumer in 39-05. What remains incomplete is the dispatch contract: the validator can approve a different path from the one the child resolves, and some embedded values never reach it.

##### Strengths

- The helper separates rejection from display: it checks before resolution, compares path components, and redacts before escaping the diagnostic copy. The existing redactor preserves non-matching text, and the planned synthetic token matches its actual pattern. [39-03-PLAN.md:193–209,243–245](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-03-PLAN.md#L193), `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/secret_shapes.py:17–22,99–114`
- The fixture is derived from explicit SPEC ranges, not from the function under test. 39-05 depends on 39-03 and checks the same pair format against the Node rule. No declared same-wave write overlap with 39-04. [39-03-PLAN.md:5–12,129–160](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-03-PLAN.md#L129), [39-05-PLAN.md:5–9,135–144](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-05-PLAN.md#L135), [39-04-PLAN.md:5–10](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-04-PLAN.md#L5)

##### Concerns

- **HIGH — The path checked is not necessarily the path the child uses.** Task 1 resolves every relative value from the Git top level. The dispatch sends the prompt unchanged and launches from the configured working directory, which can be a subdirectory. A basename that denotes a regular file at the top level can denote a different file under the child’s working directory. The existing fixture only tests the case where the two bases coincide. [39-03-PLAN.md:32,204–205,239](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-03-PLAN.md#L204), [39-12-PLAN.md:146,150–152,238–240](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-12-PLAN.md#L146), `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/runner_dispatch.py:635–655`
- **HIGH — The R8 handoff still misses actual embedded inputs.** The plan requires a missing list to reach the validator as missing; 39-12 defaults both dispatch flags to `[]` and always enumerates them. It also passes `target_dir` into soft-confinement prose without checking that value. The shipped helper interpolates it twice; `_refuse_candidate` only checks that it is a directory. Thus the R8 missing-list edge is helper-only, and the prohibition on embedding a rejected path is not covered for execute confinement text. The adapter reaches the same boundary without forwarding structured files. [39-03-PLAN.md:32,240](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-03-PLAN.md#L32), [39-12-PLAN.md:151,207,238–241](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-12-PLAN.md#L151), `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/execute_dispatch.py:39–48,106–118`, `skills/ai-kit-spec-execute-superpowers/ai_kit_spec_superpowers/dispatch_injection.py:386–397`
- **MEDIUM — Task 2’s no-real-Git instruction contradicts its verification command.** The root unit test correctly injects a fake, but the mandated hardcoding arch module runs real `git ls-files` to discover files. A strict executor cannot both run the named verification and obey the objective literally. [39-03-PLAN.md:166,238,250–259](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-03-PLAN.md#L250), `tests/test_arch_hardcoded_models.py:203–222,396–397`
- **MEDIUM — 39-10 consumes this API without a dependency path to 39-03.** Wave ordering puts it later, but its only dependency is 39-07, whose predecessors do not require 39-03. The plan imports `escape_value` directly; the shipped external delegation step checks readiness before wave execution. [39-10-PLAN.md:5–6,118–126](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-10-PLAN.md#L118), [39-07-PLAN.md:5–6](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-07-PLAN.md#L5), `.claude/gsd-core/workflows/execute-phase.md:439–448`

##### Suggestions

- Specify a single path interpretation for validation and dispatch: ground relative values from the child’s actual base while checking containment against the repository root, or make the callers supply absolute values before constructing the original prompt. Pin this with a nested-working-directory fixture.
- In 39-12, distinguish an explicitly empty file list from missing required metadata, and validate the target-directory value before interpolating guidance. Add a real-composer, no-spawn execute test for the latter. Add the explicit 39-10 dependency.
- Narrow the test rule to “no real Git in the validator fixtures”; explicitly allow read-only repository discovery in the named arch gate. Use the existing offline lint-wrapper pattern rather than asking the executor to notice a download after `uv run` starts. [39-02-PLAN.md:140](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-02-PLAN.md#L140), `Makefile:98–113`

##### Risk Assessment

**HIGH.** The helper’s tests are useful, but they do not prove confinement for the paths actually embedded and interpreted by dispatch. The implementation size is reasonable for two tasks; the larger risk is a green helper suite masking a broken caller contract.

##### Ponytail findings

- **HIGH — Task 1:** Keep one validator and reuse `gsd_tools.project_root` for repository discovery, but do not also use that root as an implicit working directory. Fix the existing caller seam instead of adding another path validator. `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/gsd_tools.py:34–48`, [39-12-PLAN.md:150–152](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-12-PLAN.md#L150)
- **LOW — Task 2:** The independent fixture is warranted: it checks two implementations without a new dependency. Keep the fixture and fake Git seam; clarify the arch-gate exception instead of wrapping or replacing Git. [39-03-PLAN.md:129–160,235–238](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-03-PLAN.md#L129), [39-05-PLAN.md:144](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-05-PLAN.md#L144)

#### 39-06

##### Summary

The accessor and refusal record largely implement the locked R7 policy: live override first, no cap on an unknown window, equality passes, and exit 65 is separate from runner statuses. The main gap is in the handoff from final argv to the accessor. The planned test starts with a fabricated context report line, so it cannot catch a real final argv carrying a context form without that line.

##### Strengths

- The plan reuses the existing catalog reader pattern, suffix handling, public runtime-row lookup, and positive-integer check. The last one really does exclude booleans, and the validity of the cap is kept separate from the validity of its provenance label. [39-06-PLAN.md:129–142,166–170](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-06-PLAN.md#L129), `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/runner_dispatch.py:247–262`, `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/usability.py:45–57`, `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/catalog_context.py:9–10,68–72`
- The `--describe` checks are tied to real runner data, not a copied table. The runner exposes reasons, classes, findings and status exits in the shapes the plan expects, and 65 is currently unused. 39-11 supplies the lens-note disjointness check. `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/references/cross-ai.mjs:1506–1529,6872–6884`, `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/runner_facts.py:30–44`, [39-11-PLAN.md:202](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-11-PLAN.md#L202)

##### Concerns

- **HIGH — An explicit context-bearing model ID can be capped by the bare model’s window.** The runner’s context handler returns before checking `id_carries` when no separate context setting was requested. The Cursor formula still places the bracketed ID on argv, and the current lane-spec builder sets `context=None`. The planned Python reader then sees no context record, passes `False`, and 39-06 strips the bracket before lookup. That can produce a pre-spawn refusal where R7 requires `unknown`. The synthetic line test does not cover this normal path; 39-07’s real-runner tests cover skill and MCP forms instead. [39-06-PLAN.md:31,140](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-06-PLAN.md#L31), `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/references/cross-ai.mjs:1764–1783,2027–2043`, `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/runner_dispatch.py:91–105`, [39-07-PLAN.md:124–132](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-07-PLAN.md#L124), [39-12-PLAN.md:195](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-12-PLAN.md#L195)
- **HIGH — The edit-pass handoff budgets before adding the capability block, then spawns without rechecking.** The plan adds lens bytes to the edit prompt budget, but the final block is appended later and the combined prompt goes directly to the runner. A lens can fit the draft and overflow the known window after the block is added. This leaves R7’s final-ordering edge uncovered for the new execute-role pass. [39-14-PLAN.md:126](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-14-PLAN.md#L126), [39-12-PLAN.md:204](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-12-PLAN.md#L204), [39-SPEC.md:565–570](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-SPEC.md#L565)
- **MEDIUM — The Node preflight does not prove the in-test checks ran.** Task 3 probes Node outside the test module, then allows the module to skip. The test runner builds a different environment for the module and suppresses successful-module detail; the named failure signal, “`TestRefusalDisjoint` reported as skipped,” will not appear on a passing module summary. [39-06-PLAN.md:197–210](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-06-PLAN.md#L197), `tools/run_unittest_parallel.py:584–601,935–943`
- **MEDIUM — The same-wave files are disjoint, but the gate can read 39-05’s in-progress template.** 39-06 runs `tests.test_runner_describe` and the new contract checks against `cross-ai.mjs` while 39-05 edits it. The checks need no *finished* 39-05 output, so there is no semantic dependency. They do need a stable template; on a shared checkout, the `<verify>` commands can fail on an intermediate edit. 39-05 also runs `tests.test_prompt_guard` while 39-06 changes it. [39-06-PLAN.md:5–9,197–210](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-06-PLAN.md#L197), [39-05-PLAN.md:5–9,153–161](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-05-PLAN.md#L153), `tests/test_runner_describe.py:15–37`. There is no comparable file overlap with wave peers 39-07, 39-08, or 39-09. [39-07-PLAN.md:5–11](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-07-PLAN.md#L5), [39-08-PLAN.md:5–11](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-08-PLAN.md#L5), [39-09-PLAN.md:5–10](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-09-PLAN.md#L5)

##### Suggestions

- Have the runner provide context evidence for an ID-carried form even without a separate setting request. Add a fake-host, real-runner test for a bracketed ID with a cached bare-model window, both with and without a live override.
- Make the edit pass reuse the final-fit check **before** claiming a pending record. Test a lens that fits before the block but not after it.
- Replace the external preflight with an in-module assertion, as 39-07 already does, or check module reports for skips if skipping is intentional on developer machines. Require isolated checkouts or serialize the overlapping verification against committed inputs. [39-07-PLAN.md:135](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-07-PLAN.md#L135), `tools/run_unittest_parallel.py:598–601`

##### Risk Assessment

**HIGH.** The accessor itself is narrow, and the two-file, three-task split fits the intended execution scale. The gaps are at the edges that determine whether a lane actually runs: incomplete context evidence can falsely refuse a lane, while the edit pass can start with an oversized final prompt.

##### Ponytail findings

- **MEDIUM — Task 1:** The accessor and refusal record are needed. Put the integer bytes-to-tokens calculation in one helper used by both lens selection and final dispatch; as written, the tested text helper is bypassed by separate calculations in 39-11 and 39-12. [39-06-PLAN.md:137–142](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-06-PLAN.md#L137), [39-11-PLAN.md:141](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-11-PLAN.md#L141), [39-12-PLAN.md:204](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-12-PLAN.md#L204)
- **HIGH — Task 2:** Keep the local invalid-value and provenance fixtures. The context-form case needs one real runner-to-composer check, not more synthetic variations of the same report line. `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/references/cross-ai.mjs:2027–2043`, [39-06-PLAN.md:166–171](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-06-PLAN.md#L166)
- **MEDIUM — Task 3:** Keep the existing supervised `read_runner_describe` helper and remove the separate Node probe script if the test can fail directly. That makes the required signal visible in the gate that matters. `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/runner_facts.py:30–44`, [39-06-PLAN.md:201–210](.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-06-PLAN.md#L201)

##### Status: Issues Found — fix and re-invoke

Reviewed **3/3** plans. This was a read-only source review: no files changed, no suites or live hosts ran, and task runtimes were not measured.

#### Batch B (39-02, 39-05, 39-17)

#### 39-02

##### Summary
The runner is the right place to derive capabilities, and the plan preserves the existing stdout contract. Two issues block execution as written: one test expects a diagnostic the proposed code cannot emit, and the capability parser can report verified facts that the final argv does not establish.

##### Strengths
- The capabilities line goes to stderr after the skill report, leaving the three-field stdout line untouched. The placement follows the existing report path in `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/references/cross-ai.mjs:7044–7056`, and the object-label format matches the consumer in `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-07-PLAN.md:131–134`.
- Both runner commits regenerate the describe golden alongside the changes that affect it. That is necessary because `tests/test_runner_describe.py:407–420` compares the entire generated output. The existing render-sync test also compares stderr, so the new line is covered without a second sync mechanism (`tests/test_cross_ai_render_sync.py:77–102,260–276`).

##### Concerns
- **HIGH — Task 2’s prescribed code and test disagree.** The helper records the first *row fact* it visits, not the first argument in argv. The Codex row lists `--sandbox` before the bypass flag, so case (m) produces a source starting `--sandbox and --dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox disagree`; the test requires the reverse. The executor has to choose which instruction to break before it can commit. See `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-02-PLAN.md:152,185,191`.
- **HIGH — Tool facts can be falsely certain.** The proposed parser scans tokens without consuming option values or respecting argument boundaries. It also treats a missing tool-list operand as an explicit empty list. Separately, its tool rules say Copilot has no shell when *either* shell name is denied, even if another explicitly allowed shell name remains; they ignore Claude’s documented deny flags entirely. Those are distinct from the correctly pinned default review profiles. See `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-02-PLAN.md:148–152,192`; `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/references/cross-ai.mjs:5359–5361`; `tests/fixtures/cli_help/claude/help.txt:81–84`; `tests/fixtures/cli_help/copilot/help.txt:97–98,112–116`. This leaves the R3 encoding/ordering edges and the no-invented-capability prohibition only partly covered.
- **MEDIUM — The argv-file acceptance test stops short of the wire.** The pure case passes a synthetic row straight to `laneCapabilities`. The render-sync test does run a real `--argv-file` preview, but only checks that template and copy agree; both could agree while omitting the line or reporting the same wrong values. See `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-02-PLAN.md:39,185,245–247`; `tests/test_cross_ai_render_sync.py:60–74,260–276`. The unknown-runtime fallback, by contrast, has a named owner in 39-07.
- **MEDIUM — No file overlap with 39-01, but Task 3 assumes exclusive use of HEAD.** `git log --oneline -2` can fail the precondition if 39-01 commits between Tasks 1 and 3 on a shared checkout. Also, Tasks 1 and 2 stage the shared describe golden; the prior run shows that file triggering the full unit/arch gate, so the 1,800-second fit is not established by the five-file scope. See `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-02-PLAN.md:156,218,239`; `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-01-PLAN.md:5–12,91`; `tools/staged_mapper.py:261–289`; `tmp/run-2026-11-19/p381-12/commit-t1.log:25`.

##### Suggestions
- Specify one conflict-diagnostic order. Make each supported flag rule distinguish a real option, its operand, and malformed or ambiguous use. Add focused cases for a missing operand, a deny that leaves another shell available, and Claude’s documented deny flag. Use `unknown` where the recorded help does not establish precedence. Correct the threat table’s “last occurrence wins” claim at `39-02-PLAN.md:282`.
- Add a real argv-file assertion to the existing `TestSkillForms.test_argv_file_lane` pattern (`tests/test_runner_skills_argv.py:183–198`). Pin one stderr capabilities line and the four `unknown` values.
- Record and check each task’s commit SHA as an ancestor, rather than requiring adjacent HEAD commits. Preflight the covering-hook time and give the orchestrator an explicit handoff if the task cannot finish inside the lane cap.

##### Risk Assessment
**HIGH.** The literal plan fails its own Task 2 test, and the parser feeds downstream tool guidance and write-lane selection. Fix both before dispatching implementation.

##### Ponytail findings
- **HIGH — Task 1:** The tracer is needed for R3. Keep it in the existing preview/report branch, but replace the two token scans with one small, operand-aware pass over the supported flag forms. Reuse the runner’s existing argument/proof patterns (`cross-ai.mjs:2132–2150,5364–5386`); do not build a general CLI parser.
- **MEDIUM — Task 2:** The six-host matrix is required and the existing fake-runner pattern is a good fit (`cross-ai-selftest.mjs:2885–2904,10191–10212`). Put the unusual flag inputs in the pure cases rather than making the subprocess matrix larger.
- **LOW — Task 3:** Keep the locked Python integration check and reuse `_SkillCase._run` (`tests/test_runner_skills_argv.py:46–86`). Extend the existing argv-file fixture instead of creating another harness or test module.

#### 39-05

##### Summary
This is a narrow implementation of the amended R6 requirement. It retains the existing error messages and root-check ownership, and its shared fixture is a strong drift check. The remaining gaps are in what the tests prove and how the task runs in wave 3, not in the proposed Unicode set.

##### Strengths
- The two checks cover different inputs: `parseArgs` rejects the supplied path and `loadSkills` rejects a clean-looking link whose resolved directory contains a disallowed character. The latter check is before the skill body read. See `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-05-PLAN.md:109–116,141–149`; `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/references/cross-ai.mjs:6485–6499,6694–6700`.
- The fixture is generated independently from the locked ranges and checked exhaustively by both languages. The existing Python skill test already covers order and duplicate directories, including a symlink alias. See `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-03-PLAN.md:129–160,235–236`; `tests/test_runner_skills_argv.py:170–181`.

##### Concerns
- **MEDIUM — The new refusal cases do not prove that diagnostics omit the rejected input.** They check for the generic message, but would also pass if a second stderr line echoed the rejected path. The current runner has separate parse and load error paths, and the latter strips only the old control set from error text. The user’s no-raw-value rule needs a negative assertion at both boundaries. See `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-05-PLAN.md:109,142–147`; `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/references/cross-ai.mjs:6920–6923,6969–6972`.
- **MEDIUM — Wave 3 has no declared file overlap, but two checks assume serialization.** Task 2 demands that Task 1 be the latest commit and runs `tests.test_prompt_guard`, which same-wave 39-06 is editing. In a shared checkout, another wave-3 commit can invalidate the first check, and an in-progress 39-06 edit can invalidate the second. This is not a missing dependency on 39-06’s *feature*: the R6 test only needs 39-03’s committed fixture and validator. See `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-05-PLAN.md:5–9,135,161`; `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-06-PLAN.md:5–9,197–204`. The other wave-3 plans, 39-07, 39-08 and 39-09, do not declare edits to this plan’s two files.

##### Suggestions
- Assert that stderr does not contain the supplied or resolved invalid value. Reuse the existing no-leak assertion pattern in `cross-ai-selftest.mjs:11745`. Keep the old MCP scope; do not broaden R6 to unrelated flags.
- Check the Task 1 commit by SHA ancestry. Run wave peers in isolated worktrees or serialize shared-checkout verification. Add a 39-11 handoff fixture with a clean symlink to a disallowed directory name: the expected result is a named `path_rejected` lens skip, not runner exit 2. The plan describes that precheck, but the listed fixtures do not pin this exact handoff (`.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-11-PLAN.md:140,197`).

##### Risk Assessment
**MEDIUM.** The implementation is small and well bounded. The main risks are a preventable wave stop and incomplete proof at the diagnostic and Python-to-runner boundaries.

##### Ponytail findings
- **LOW — Task 1:** The parse check is required. Extend the existing usage-case pattern (`cross-ai-selftest.mjs:9125–9137`) rather than adding more wrappers or duplicating the empty-path regression.
- **MEDIUM — Task 2:** The resolved-directory check is necessary; the shared-set test earns its place. The extra skill-name regex check is behaviorally redundant with the existing ASCII grammar (`cross-ai.mjs:6614–6617,6694`), but explicitly using the same set satisfies the locked contract. Keep it at the existing parse site and test the handoff, not a new abstraction.

#### 39-17

##### Summary
The version bump, live render, and timing refresh are correctly separated. The pinned dry-run approval and native timing checkpoint are useful. The live publish still has a gap between the approved bytes and the bytes the installer is authorized to replace, and Task 3 has a concrete environment prerequisite missing from the plan.

##### Strengths
- Task 1 keeps the version pins and generated goldens together while leaving installer readiness at 9. The installer tests readiness independently of the shipped version (`skills/ai-kit-gsd-curated-config/ai_kit_gsd_curated_config/cross_ai_script.py:314–329`), and the existing docs tests pin the shipped version (`tests/test_cross_ai_skill_text.py:396–409,442–448`).
- The timing plan uses the shipped recorder, exact case-set comparison, and per-module reports rather than trusting an `ok` line that could hide a skip. See `tests/cross_ai_selftest.py:324–351`, `tmp/run-2026-11-19/p37-23/check_times.py:23–35`, and `tmp/run-2026-11-19/p381-12/check_reports.py:14–33,63–73`. Wave 11 has no peer; the transitive dependency chain reaches both runner plans, and 39-18 directly depends on 39-17.

##### Concerns
- **HIGH — The approval is checked outside the operation that publishes.** After Task 2 checks the live digest, `render.py live` asks `ensure-runner` to plan from whatever bytes it reads next. The installer’s `expected` bytes come from that new read, not the approved snapshot. If an intervening update is pristine, the installer can replace it before the dry-digest mismatch is detected. The `.new` branch protects a change noticed *after installer planning*; it does not protect the approval-to-planning interval, and the installer documents a final re-read-to-replace race too. See `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-17-PLAN.md:152,159–165`; `tmp/run-2026-11-19/p381-12/render.py:153–168`; `skills/ai-kit-gsd-curated-config/ai_kit_gsd_curated_config/cross_ai_script.py:265–289,358–388`.
- **MEDIUM — Task 3 can stop on a machine that already has a usable Node.** It copies `record.sh` unchanged. That script requires `VOLTA_HOME` unconditionally, even without a Volta shim; the plan gives neither a precondition nor a way to resolve the real Node before replacing HOME. Task 1 already has the latter helper. Also, `check_times.py` is invoked outside the scratch wrapper, and its `list_cases()` creates a temporary directory under the inherited `TMPDIR`, contrary to the task’s isolation rule. See `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-17-PLAN.md:96,132,193–203`; `tmp/run-2026-11-19/p381-12/record.sh:8–16`; `tests/cross_ai_selftest.py:39–52,216–229`.
- **MEDIUM — Task 1 exceeds the lane’s file-count guideline and repeats a known costly hook path.** The 15-path commit is justified, but the previous run did this task natively and the shared describe golden triggered a full unit/arch run. Task 3 is explicitly native; Task 1 has no equivalent routing decision or measured cap check. See `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-17-PLAN.md:119–137,183–186`; `.planning/phases/38.1-runner-error-classification-and-retry-policy-inserted/38.1-12-SUMMARY.md:3–9,15–26`; `tmp/run-2026-11-19/p381-12/commit-t1.log:25,207`.
- **MEDIUM — The material skill change is not pinned at the judge handoff.** Task 1 adds a capabilities contract and raises the documented caller floor, but plans no eval for reading the new line or handling its absence. The existing cross-AI evals cover other runner behaviors. Also, the receiving plan’s fix gate names `tests.test_lane_capability_docs`; 39-15 actually puts those checks in `tests/test_cross_ai_docs_text.py`. A judge fix can therefore stop on an import error. See `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-17-PLAN.md:120,133,261`; `skills/ai-kit-cross-ai/evals/evals.json:4–43`; `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-18-PLAN.md:117,150`; `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-15-PLAN.md:169–175`.

##### Suggestions
- Bind both the expected target bytes and the proposed output digest to the publish step, before writing. Use an exclusive maintenance window if the installer cannot close the final race, and document that limit. Keep the stop-without-overwrite behavior for ambiguous ownership. A post-write check is useful evidence, not authorization.
- Route Task 1 natively upfront, or demonstrate that the full task *including its hook* fits the cap. Keep the pins in one commit. For Task 3, reuse Task 1’s real-Node resolution, set a scratch `TMPDIR` for the timing checker, and run lint with the same offline policy as the runner plans.
- Require the judge handoff to include `ai-kit-cross-ai` as materially changed; record a separate disposition for curated-config’s number-only edit. Add one focused capability-preview eval and point the receiving gate at the actual doc-test module.

##### Risk Assessment
**HIGH.** This is the only plan that replaces the live runner. The main blocker is preserving the owner’s approved bytes at publication; the missing runtime preflight and judge-test reference are additional avoidable stops.

##### Ponytail findings
- **MEDIUM — Task 1:** The atomic pin update is required, not scope creep. Reuse the golden writers (`tests/test_runner_describe.py:413–420`; `tests/test_curated_config_golden.py:218–229`) and keep the large mechanical commit intact. Add only the missing behavior pin and eval.
- **HIGH — Task 2:** Reuse `ensure_runner` and its existing compare-before-write machinery (`cross_ai_script.py:358–388`). A second dry run does not close the publication gap. Put the approval check where it can stop the write.
- **MEDIUM — Task 3:** One recording and one shard measurement are enough. Reuse `record_times` and `split_cases` (`tests/cross_ai_selftest.py:245–268,324–351`), remove the unconditional Volta requirement, and keep the curated-config matrix out of this refresh as planned.

##### Phase coverage notes
Three locked behaviors still lack a complete implementation path in the handoffs: **R5 snapshot consistency** is recorded as a limit, but the runner rereads the file after Python budgets it (`39-SPEC.md:559`; `39-11-PLAN.md:31,140`; `cross-ai.mjs:6496–6503`); **R8 missing-list rejection** has a validator test, but dispatch defaults an omitted list to empty (`39-SPEC.md:572`; `39-03-PLAN.md:209`; `39-12-PLAN.md:206–207`); **R9’s no-unaccepted-edits prohibition** is not proven for edits outside the approved list, because 39-14 only snapshots and restores listed files (`39-SPEC.md:610`; `39-14-PLAN.md:118,126–127`). These are handoff gaps, not requests to reopen the locked decisions.

Reviewed all three manifest plans against the checkout at `9e3f11e`. No files were changed; no suites, live host calls, or timing runs were performed.

#### Batch C (39-04, 39-08, 39-09)

#### 39-04

**Summary.** The plan gives R1 a clear owner for disk-based presence, role resolution, and the `skill-table` CLI. It assigns all seven R1 edge rows, including the precision backstop. The main gaps are at the file-read boundary and in the handoff to dispatch: a scan that handles a bad seed or roles config does not help if the next caller rereads the same input and raises.

**Strengths**
- The tracer distinguishes seed data from presence and adds a genuine rescan test: create and remove a skill between calls, with no cache invalidation. Keeping empty roots in `roots` also gives R8 a stable allowlist independent of whether a root contains a skill. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-04-PLAN.md:125–129,209–218`
- Reusing the detection file map and parser avoids a second MCP config map. The fixtures distinguish absent files from malformed files and check that server values do not reach JSON. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-04-PLAN.md:205–216`; `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/detection.py:1397–1435`

**Concerns**
- **HIGH — Task 3 can block a review or execute lane before preview.** The plan calls `os.stat` but discards the file type, then passes the path to a blocking, unbounded `open().read()`. A non-regular config file can hang the scan; a large file can consume unbounded memory. Testing a directory only exercises the immediate error case. The existing reader was reused correctly for parsing, but not hardened for a scan on every dispatch. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-04-PLAN.md:207,216`; `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/detection.py:1425–1435`
- **HIGH — The cycle-1 fallback is not carried through to lane dispatch.** Task 1 catches seed and roles errors and keeps scanning. But 39-11 calls `load_seed()` again in both lens selection and MCP selection, and 39-12 calls `user_roles()` again before the MCP pick. A bad seed or roles file can therefore still stop composition. The R1 no-block prohibition has a scan-level fixture but lacks a failing-input test at the dispatch boundary. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-04-PLAN.md:128–141`; `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-11-PLAN.md:138,171`; `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-12-PLAN.md:201–205`
- **MEDIUM — Redaction covers MCP names, not the complete output.** Task 1 prints raw skill names, paths, roots, and exception text; Task 3 embeds raw config paths in `mcp_note`. JSON escaping is not secret redaction. `ConfigError.__str__` includes the path and message, and 39-09 forwards these notes to another user-visible output. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-04-PLAN.md:139–142,216`; `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/config_io.py:296–309`; `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-09-PLAN.md:130–131`
- **MEDIUM — Task 3’s exact `scan` body exceeds the lint cap.** Combining Tasks 1–3 leaves 17 locals including arguments, against a cap of 15. The executor must invent a split after the gate fails. The verification summary also still requires `tests.test_config_doctor`, although Task 3 explicitly removed it. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-04-PLAN.md:141,183,217,223–236,263`; `pyproject.toml:75–80`
- **MEDIUM — The verification and commit commands do not enforce offline execution.** The test wrapper uses scratch HOME and the test runner supplies a host tripwire, but the bare lint targets and commit hooks invoke `uv run`. “Stop if a download starts” does not prevent the first request. The three-task plan also has enough fixture and scanner work that I would not assume the largest task plus hooks fits the 1,800-second cap without a timing check. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-04-PLAN.md:91,132,168–184,220–232`; `Makefile:98–113`; `.pre-commit-config.yaml:20–44,55–65`

**Suggestions**
- Specify a bounded, regular-file read and an `unknown` result for unsupported file types. Assign the 39-04 → 39-11/39-12 handoff one error-handled snapshot, and test that malformed optional inputs do not stop an otherwise valid lane.
- Keep raw identities for internal matching; redact and escape at CLI and inventory output boundaries. Extract the per-host assembly step explicitly, align the verification summary with the command, and give the executor an offline wrapper for lint and commit.

**Risk assessment: HIGH.** This scan runs before lane launch. A blocking file read or an uncaught reread of optional data can prevent otherwise unrelated work.

##### Ponytail findings
- **LOW — Task 1:** The R1 tracer is needed. Reuse the three-line registration pattern in `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/config_status.py:80–102`; no extra CLI layer is needed. The CLI and rescan fixtures are the right first commit. `39-04-PLAN.md:125–146`
- **MEDIUM — Task 2:** Plugin scanning and description extraction are needed for R1/R2. Mirroring the existing parser respects the import boundary, but the copy also inherits a blocking file open and accepts relative plugin install paths. Specify safe file handling here so the sorter does not have to compensate later. `39-04-PLAN.md:181–183`; `skills/ai-kit-gsd-curated-config/ai_kit_gsd_curated_config/skill_scan.py:115–120,245–261`
- **MEDIUM — Task 3:** Names-only parsing and root collection are needed; a new parser or runtime probe is not. Keep the existing parser, add the file-read guard, and pin the resulting contract at the downstream caller. `39-04-PLAN.md:205–218`; `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/detection.py:1397–1435`

#### 39-08

**Summary.** Separating advisory classification from confirmation is the right R2 design. The plan assigns all five R2 edge rows and tests the ordinary flow, including low and forced answers. The remaining issues are at the real jev boundary, at the config-write boundary, and in the missing curated-config handoff.

**Strengths**
- The request uses jev’s `context` field for third-party text, not its instructions field. The real client explicitly treats context as untrusted evidence. Passing `cwd` also makes the classifier and jev use the same project's config layer. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-08-PLAN.md:124–139`; `skills/jev-fast-decision/scripts/jev_review.py:306–319,620–626`
- The confirm path uses the existing TOML splicer and atomic publisher. The mode, readback, sequential preservation, and overlapping-confirm fixtures test more than the command's success message. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-08-PLAN.md:167–179`; `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/toml_splice.py:429–448,780–792`

**Concerns**
- **HIGH — Task 1 can read outside the scanned skill roots and disclose the resulting description to jev.** `role_item` opens the scan row’s path without checking the resolved `SKILL.md` target. 39-04’s listing intentionally follows links, so an addressable row can point to an out-of-root file. The test only shows that the path string is absent from the payload; it does not establish where the text came from. The skill name is also printed and put in the request without a secret-shape check. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-08-PLAN.md:127–140`; `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-04-PLAN.md:139,181`. The related lens reader already specifies resolved-file validation in `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-11-PLAN.md:139–140`.
- **HIGH — Task 3’s no-write test cannot prove the stated contract.** The fake never starts the real jev client. In production the ladder calls that client, whose screen path appends an answer log and records model observations. No `[skills.roles]` write is a different guarantee from “no new file under HOME.” The plan and 39-16 both say read-only without naming this distinction. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-08-PLAN.md:139,206–211`; `skills/jev-fast-decision/scripts/jev_ladder.py:589–599`; `skills/jev-fast-decision/scripts/jev_review.py:1937–1956,2335–2361`; `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-16-PLAN.md:127–134`
- **HIGH — Task 2’s confinement and locking do not cover the same target that is published.** The shared guard rejects a linked config and a link onto the user config, but it computes the allowed folder from the already-resolved `.aikit` parent. It does not establish that this parent is inside the confirmed project. The check also happens before the lock, while `publish` resolves the path again. And the new lock serializes confirms with each other, not with `render-toml`, which can publish an older snapshot of the same file. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-08-PLAN.md:171–177`; `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/config_io.py:477–491`; `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/toml_splice.py:780–792`; `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/cli.py:1022–1041`
- **MEDIUM — A failed client can still produce a proposal.** The plan accepts `reason == ""`, but `ask_items` parses stdout from nonzero exits except the specific old-client error. A failed process with a valid-looking answer passes. This does not require changing existing catalog behavior: the classifier needs a strict success contract and a fake nonzero-exit case. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-08-PLAN.md:139,205`; `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/jev_names.py:154–171`
- **MEDIUM — The documented confirmation surface cannot address every scanned MCP name.** `confirm-skill-role` uses the skill-name regex, which rejects `.` and `@`; 39-04 accepts both in MCP names, and 39-16 documents this command as the writer for skill **or MCP** roles. A name such as `srv.example` can be inventoried but not confirmed as documented. Also, Task 2’s import replacement does not match the import line Task 1 leaves after adding `secret_shapes`. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-08-PLAN.md:132,138,176–178`; `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-04-PLAN.md:216`; `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-16-PLAN.md:147`
- **MEDIUM — The test-file disposition does not match the execution rules.** 39-08 creates `skill_classify.py` but adds its tests to `test_skill_table.py`, despite the explicit per-module test rule. Its stated reason is avoiding generated listings, but none of the other wave-3 plans owns those listings. Task 3 also inherits the bare `uv run` lint and hook path noted under 39-04. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-08-PLAN.md:7–11,143,212–224`; `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-05-PLAN.md:7–9`; `39-06-PLAN.md:7–9`; `39-07-PLAN.md:7–11`; `39-09-PLAN.md:7–10`; `Makefile:98–113`

**Suggestions**
- Specify the checked description source, reject unsafe names before display or request construction, and require a successful client exit before accepting an advisory result. Resolve the write guarantee explicitly: either arrange for jev not to persist state on this route, or obtain an explicit amendment narrowing “read-only” to configuration files and document the client logging.
- Bind confinement, locking, reading, and publishing to the approved target. Test an externally linked parent and a competing config update. Reconcile the MCP-name contract, then give Task 2 the literal import and Task 3 the offline invocation it should use.

**Risk assessment: HIGH.** The feature is optional, but it sends local text to a separate decision client and writes the owner's layered config. The planned tests do not yet establish either boundary fully.

##### Ponytail findings
- **MEDIUM — Task 1:** Reuse `jev_names.find_jev` and its existing batch/answer logic (`skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/jev_names.py:63–69,106–171`). The extra `user_roles()` read in front of `scan()` is not needed if the same scan can expose a definitive error to this strict caller. That also avoids a disagreement between two reads. `39-08-PLAN.md:135–139`
- **HIGH — Task 2:** The lock is needed; atomic replacement alone does not protect a read–modify–write. Reuse the splicer and the existing linked-parent regression pattern, but test the actual approved target and not just another call to the same helper. `39-08-PLAN.md:167–177`; `skills/ai-kit-spec-review/ai_kit_spec/model_catalog.py:375–388`; `tests/test_config_layers.py:369–381`
- **MEDIUM — Task 3:** Keep the absent/low/forced fixtures. Add the missing trust-boundary cases rather than more success-path repetitions. The existing fake pattern in `tests/test_catalog_jev_names.py:110–146` is enough for response parsing; it is not evidence about client filesystem side effects. `39-08-PLAN.md:199–215`

#### 39-09

**Summary.** The inventory field is additive, the test freezes the old visibility output before the change, and the plan correctly distinguishes GSD’s five visibility hosts from the shared scanner’s seven entries. All five R10 edge rows are assigned, but the acceptance case for a missing review package remains unowned. Task 2 also does not instruct the executor to implement one of its own promised tests.

**Strengths**
- Keeping GSD’s root mapping is necessary: its Codex root maps to `.agents/skills`, while the shared scan exposes that root separately. The frozen fixture exercises the mapping and both visibility paths. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-09-PLAN.md:119–129`; `tests/test_curated_config_golden.py:106–125`
- The existing loader preserves the frozen import boundary, and the optional field does not require a schema change. The validator checks required fields and the existing visibility lists rather than rejecting extra top-level fields. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-09-PLAN.md:130–132`; `skills/ai-kit-gsd-curated-config/ai_kit_gsd_curated_config/gsd_catalog.py:28–42`; `skills/ai-kit-gsd-curated-config/ai_kit_gsd_curated_config/plan_io.py:75–106,181–213`

**Concerns**
- **HIGH — The missing-package R10 acceptance case still fails.** Task 2 mocks only `skill_table` as unavailable. With the review sibling actually missing, `resolve_gsd_core` returns an error before `_skill_roles` is reached; fixing that first dependency alone would still leave catalog loading dependent on the sibling. The plan acknowledges the deferral but names no implementing plan, while the closing plan carries REV-05. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-09-PLAN.md:28–31,155`; `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-SPEC.md:418–428`; `skills/ai-kit-gsd-curated-config/ai_kit_gsd_curated_config/host.py:122–130`; `skills/ai-kit-gsd-curated-config/ai_kit_gsd_curated_config/catalog_adapter.py:69–75`; `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-19-PLAN.md:6–10`
- **MEDIUM — The malformed-role fix is not executable as claimed.** The triage says `_skill_roles` catches scan exceptions, but Task 1 calls `table.scan` directly. Task 2 describes `test_malformed_roles_config_warns` yet its action and acceptance check omit that test. Its `<done>` also says malformed roles leave `skill_roles` absent, the opposite of the expected warning-with-field result. The artifact signature omits `home`, while the real replacement uses it for fallback. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-09-PLAN.md:38–39,129–131,155–179,212–214`
- **MEDIUM — Visibility reuses a helper, not the inventory’s skill rows.** Task 1 builds `skill_roles`, then lists GSD roots again with empty seed and role maps. Mapping Codex to the GSD root justifies different host keys; it does not require relisting a root already present in the scan. As written the two fields can disagree on presence within one inventory call, and the fixture only tests stable disk state. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-09-PLAN.md:124,129–131`; `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-04-PLAN.md:139–141,218`
- **LOW — Task 2’s required gate list and reported evidence differ.** The executable command includes `tests.test_curated_config_golden`; the verification summary omits it. Retain the golden check: those tests intentionally mask the inventory digest and check plan content, which is the relevant distinction for an additive field. As in the other plans, run the lint and hook paths offline rather than relying on the pre-provisioned environment alone. `.planning/phases/39-lane-capability-blocks-and-lens-skills/39-09-PLAN.md:161–173,203`; `tests/test_curated_config_golden.py:210–217,305–319`; `Makefile:98–113`

**Suggestions**
- Assign the orchestrator an explicit choice before closeout: cover the full missing-sibling path with an inventory CLI fixture, or obtain an owner-approved amendment limiting R10 to a missing optional module. Do not report the narrower fixture as the broader acceptance result.
- Spell out the malformed-config test in the action and make the artifacts, completion criteria, and verification list agree. Reuse scanned rows for matching GSD roots; specify a fresh listing only for GSD roots the shared scan did not cover.

**Risk assessment: HIGH for R10 closeout; MEDIUM for installed users.** The normal installed path is small and leaves the schema intact. The missing-package case is a source-confirmed failure against a locked acceptance criterion.

##### Ponytail findings
- **MEDIUM — Task 1:** The loader, additive field, and old-output freeze are necessary. Reuse `load_ai_kit_spec_module` and the existing inventory CLI fixture. Avoid a second scan for roots already covered, but keep the GSD root mapping instead of forcing the two host tables to match. `39-09-PLAN.md:124–132`; `skills/ai-kit-gsd-curated-config/ai_kit_gsd_curated_config/gsd_catalog.py:28–42`; `tests/test_curated_config_inventory.py:163–178`

Keep separate assertions for missing module, missing sibling, and malformed roles. `39-09-PLAN.md:155–164`; `tests/test_curated_config_inventory.py:133–178`
